## 一、我对图意的理解

**驱动变量**：一个全局参数 θ 随时间增加，所有运动都由它派生。状态栏实时显示 `θ = x.xxxπ`。**底空间（两栏共用）**：x = e^{iθ} 在单位圆上**逆时针**转动。**左栏：分开不粘合**

- A 叶（普通 t 复平面）：t₁ = e^{iθ/2}，红点，**逆时针**、半速转动。
- B 叶（另一张普通 t 复平面）：t₂ = e^{i(2π+θ)/2} = −t₁，蓝点，同样**逆时针**半速，始终与 t₁ 关于原点对径。
- 两叶之间是波浪撕裂线，表示互不相干；信息框说明 t = ±√x 在底空间上只是二值对应、不是单值函数。

**右栏：交叉粘合（关键在 B 叶）**

- A 叶与左栏完全一样：t₁ = e^{iθ/2}，逆时针。
- **B 叶的横轴做了镜像反转**——这就是你说的“下面那个单位圆的 x 轴反向”。于是 t₂ 的真实值 e^{i(2π+θ)/2} 被显示成它关于纵轴的镜像，角度变成 −θ/2：蓝点从 0° 出发**顺时针**走，正好和底空间 x 的逆时针相反。
- 效果：θ = 0 时 t₁、t₂ 都在右侧 0°；θ 增大时红点上行、蓝点下行；θ = 2π 时两者在左侧 180° 处“会师”（跨越粘合缝的时刻）；θ = 4π 时同时回到起点。一圈换支、两圈还原，单值化一目了然。
- 两叶之间画虚线粘合缝，左端蓝圈标注“A 的下边界粘合到 B 的上边界”，右端红圈标注“A 的上边界粘合到 B 的下边界”，并标 Riemann Surface；信息框写 X = {(x,t) : x = t²}，π 是 2 重分支覆盖、t 是单值全纯函数。

**周期**：θ 从 0 跑到 4π 是一个完整循环，之后无缝循环播放。

## 二、动画设计计划

1. **布局**：单个 HTML 文件，零依赖，用 Canvas 2D 绘制。桌面端左右两栏并排对比；窄屏自动变为上下堆叠。每栏结构 = 标题 → A 叶面板 → 撕裂线／粘合缝 → B 叶面板 → 底空间小圆 → 信息框；页面底部是实时状态栏。
2. **逐帧绘制**：每个面板画单位圆、带箭头的坐标轴、原点“0”、从原点到小球的半径连线、小球（带光晕）、弧形方向箭头（左栏两叶都逆时针；右栏 A 逆时针、B 顺时针）、标签 A / B / t₁ / t₂ / x / 底空间。
3. **增强表达**（截图里没有、动画才能做的）：
   
   - 小球拖一段淡淡的**轨迹尾迹**，让“顺时针 vs 逆时针”不靠箭头也能直接看出来；
   - θ 经过 2π（跨缝时刻）时粘合缝虚线短暂高亮，提示“换支”发生。
4. **交互**：播放／暂停、速度调节、θ 拖动条（可手动 scrub 到任意相位对比两栏）。

```js
const state = { theta: 0, playing: true, speed: 1 };
const BASE_SPEED = 0.3 * Math.PI; // rad/s

function loop(ts) { dt; if playing theta += BASE_SPEED*speed*dt; theta %= 4π; update UI; draw both columns; requestAnimationFrame }
```

```js
function drawColumn(canvas, mode) { // mode 'separate' | 'glued'
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  // size handled in resize()
  const w = canvas._w, s = w / 520; // scale
  ctx.clearRect... 
  // layout
  const padX = 14*s;
  const panelA = {x:padX, y:10*s, w:w-2*padX, h:300*s};
  const seamY0 = panelA.y + panelA.h; const seamH = 52*s;
  const panelB = {x:padX, y:seamY0+seamH, w:w-2*padX, h:300*s};
  const baseY0 = panelB.y + panelB.h; const baseH = 225*s;
  canvas height = baseY0 + baseH + 6*s.
}
```

```js
function drawExpFormula(ctx, x, y, baseStr, supStr, color, align='left', fontScale=s) {
  const f1 = `italic ${18*fontScale}px "Times New Roman", serif`;
  const f2 = `italic ${13*fontScale}px "Times New Roman", serif`;

  ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.textBaseline='middle';
  ctx.font=f1; ctx.fillText(baseStr, x0, y);
  ctx.font=f2; ctx.fillText(supStr, x0+wBase, y-8*fontScale);
}
```

```html
  .info .math { font-style:italic; }

  .caption { margin:12px 8px 0; font-size:14px; line-height:1.6; color:#444; }
  #status { max-width:1240px; margin:0 auto 26px; padding:0 14px; }
  #status .bar { border:1.5px solid #333; border-radius:10px; background:#fff; padding:10px 16px; font-size:15px; line-height:1.6; }
  @media (max-width: 900px) { main { flex-direction:column; } }
</style>

<body>
<header>
  <h1>The Two Branches of √x — Separate Sheets vs Cross-Glued Riemann Surface</h1>
  <p>x = e<sup>iθ</sup> winds around the origin of the base space; watch t₁ and t₂ on the sheets above.</p>
</header>
<div id="controls">
  <button id="playBtn">Pause</button>

  <section class="col" id="colL">
    <h2>Separate, Not Glued<small>two independent copies of the t-plane</small></h2>
    <canvas id="cvL"></canvas>
    <div class="info">
      On the base space Ĉ<sub>x</sub>,<br>
      <span class="math" style="display:block;text-align:center;font-size:18px;">t = <span class="red">+</span> <span class="blue">−</span> √x</span>
      is not a single-valued map, but a two-valued analytic correspondence (multivalued inverse):
      <span class="math" style="display:block;text-align:center;font-size:17px;">x ↝ { <span class="red">+√x</span> , <span class="blue">−√x</span> }.</span>
    </div>
    <p class="caption">The two branches are placed on two separate “sheets”: sheet A above, sheet B below. Both sheets are ordinary complex planes; nothing is glued.</p>
  </section>
  <section class="col" id="colR">
    <h2>Cross-Glued<small>the Riemann surface of √x</small></h2>
    <canvas id="cvR"></canvas>
    <div class="info">
      On the Riemann surface X,&nbsp; X = {(x,t) : x = t²}.<br>
      <span class="blue math">π : X → Ĉ<sub>x</sub>,&nbsp; π(x,t) = x</span><br>
      is a single-valued holomorphic map (2-fold branched cover).<br>
      <span class="red math">t : X → Ĉ<sub>t</sub>,&nbsp; (x,t) ↦ t</span><br>
      is a single-valued holomorphic function.
    </div>
    <p class="caption">Still drawn as two sheets, but they represent the cross-glued Riemann surface. Sheet B is drawn with its horizontal axis reversed, so t₂ appears to run clockwise — opposite to x in the base space.</p>
  </section>
</main>
<div id="status"><div class="bar">
  Current: <b id="thetaVal">θ = 0.000π</b>. As θ increases, x runs counterclockwise in the base space;
  t₁ follows <span class="math red">e<sup>iθ/2</sup></span>; t₂ follows <span class="math blue">e<sup>i(2π+θ)/2</sup></span>.
  In the cross-glued view, sheet B is drawn with its horizontal axis reversed, so t₂ appears clockwise.
</div></div>
<script>...</script>
</body>
</html>
```

```js
function resizeCanvas(cv) {
  const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  const w = cv.clientWidth;
  const s = w / 520;
  const h = 880 * s;
  cv.width = Math.round(w * dpr);
  cv.height = Math.round(h * dpr);
  cv.style.height = h + 'px';
  const ctx = cv.getContext('2d');
  ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
  return { ctx, w, h, s };
}
```

```js
function layout(w, s) {
  const padX = 14 * s;
  const A = { x: padX, y: 12 * s, w: w - 2 * padX, h: 296 * s };
  const seam = { y: A.y + A.h, h: 54 * s };
  const B = { x: padX, y: seam.y + seam.h, w: w - 2 * padX, h: 296 * s };
  const base = { y: B.y + B.h, h: 210 * s };
  return { A, seam, B, base, total: base.y + base.h + 8 * s };
}
```

total = 12+296+54+296+210+8 = 876 s. Good, matches 880.Helpers:

JavaScript 复制

```js
function pt(cx, cy, R, phi) { return [cx + R * Math.cos(phi), cy - R * Math.sin(phi)]; }

function line(ctx, x0, y0, x1, y1) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x0,y0); ctx.lineTo(x1,y1); ctx.stroke(); }

function arrowHead(ctx, x, y, dx, dy, size, color) {
  // dx,dy unit direction of motion; draw two barbs
  const a = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
  for (const sgn of [1,-1]) {
    const ang = a + sgn * 2.6; // barb pointing backward
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x, y);
    ctx.lineTo(x + size * Math.cos(ang), y + size * Math.sin(ang));
    ctx.stroke();
  }
}
```

arcArrow(ctx, cx, cy, r, a0, a1, color, s):

JavaScript 复制

```js
function arcArrow(ctx, cx, cy, r, a0, a1, color, s) {
  const n = 24;
  ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.lineWidth = 2 * s;
  ctx.beginPath();
  for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
    const a = a0 + (a1 - a0) * i / n;
    const [x, y] = pt(cx, cy, r, a);
    i ? ctx.lineTo(x, y) : ctx.moveTo(x, y);
  }
  ctx.stroke();
  const [x, y] = pt(cx, cy, r, a1);
  // tangent: d/da pt = (-r sin a, -r cos a) direction of increasing a
  const dir = Math.sign(a1 - a0);
  const dx = -Math.sin(a1) * dir, dy = -Math.cos(a1) * dir;
  arrowHead(ctx, x, y, dx, dy, 9 * s, color);
}
```

Check tangent: pt = (cx + r cos a, cy − r sin a); d/da = (−r sin a, −r cos a). Unit = (−sin a, −cos a). ✓ For a1=75° CCW: dir=+1, tangent = (−sin75, −cos75) = (−0.97, −0.26) → up-left ✓ (canvas y down, −0.26 means up).
```js
function drawTear(ctx, seam, w, s) {
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#111'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.4*s;
  for (const off of [-8*s, 8*s]) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    const y0 = seam.y + seam.h/2 + off;
    for (let x = 6*s; x <= w - 6*s; x += 4) {
      const y = y0 + 4.5*s * Math.sin(x / (16*s) + off);
      x === 6*s ? ctx.moveTo(x, y) : ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    }
    ctx.stroke();
  }
}
```

JavaScript 复制

```js
function drawSeam(ctx, seam, w, s, timeMs, glow) {
  const y = seam.y + seam.h / 2;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.strokeStyle = glow > 0 ? `rgba(190,40,20,${0.35 + 0.65*glow})` : '#333';
  ctx.lineWidth = (glow > 0 ? 2.4 : 1.6) * s;
  if (glow > 0) { ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(220,60,30,0.8)'; ctx.shadowBlur = 12 * glow * s; }
  ctx.setLineDash([7*s, 6*s]);
  ctx.lineDashOffset = -timeMs / 40;
  line(ctx, 10*s, y, w - 10*s, y);
  ctx.restore();
  ctx.setLineDash([]);
  // end circles
  ctx.lineWidth = 2*s;
  ctx.strokeStyle = BLUE; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(10*s, y, 6*s, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.strokeStyle = RED;  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(w - 10*s, y, 6*s, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.stroke();
  // annotations

  ctx.font = f; 
  ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.fillStyle = BLUE;
  ctx.fillText('lower edge of A', 20*s, y + 16*s);
  ctx.fillText('glued to upper edge of B', 20*s, y + 31*s);
  ctx.textAlign = 'right'; ctx.fillStyle = RED;
  ctx.fillText('upper edge of A', w - 20*s, y - 26*s);
  ctx.fillText('glued to lower edge of B', w - 20*s, y - 11*s);
}
```

Optimization: store cv._w; if clientWidth changed → resize; else just clearRect. Let me implement:

JavaScript 复制

```js
function prepare(cv) {
  const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  const w = cv.clientWidth;
  const s = w / 520;
  const h = 880 * s;
  if (cv._w !== w || cv._dpr !== dpr) {
    cv.width = Math.round(w*dpr); cv.height = Math.round(h*dpr);
    cv.style.height = h + 'px';
    cv._w = w; cv._dpr = dpr;
  }
  const ctx = cv.getContext('2d');
  ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);
  return { ctx, w, s };
}
```

Then:

JavaScript 复制

```js
function draw(mode, timeMs) {
  const cv = mode === 'sep' ? cvL : cvR;
  const { ctx, w, s } = prepare(cv);
  const L = layout(w, s);
  const glued = mode === 'glue';
  drawSheet(ctx, L.A, { s, sheetLetter:'A', color:RED, dotLabel:'t₁',
    angleFn: th => th/2, arc:[Math.PI/6, Math.PI*0.42], sup:'iθ/2',
    formulaSide:'right' });
  if (glued) drawSeam(ctx, L.seam, w, s, timeMs, glowVal()); else drawTear(ctx, L.seam, w, s);
  drawSheet(ctx, L.B, { s, sheetLetter:'B', color:BLUE, dotLabel:'t₂',
    angleFn: glued ? th => -th/2 : th => Math.PI + th/2,
    arc: glued ? [-Math.PI/6, -Math.PI*0.44] : [Math.PI*1.12, Math.PI*1.38],
    sup:'i(2π+θ)/2', formulaSide: glued ? 'left' : 'right',
    note: glued ? 'horizontal axis reversed' : null,
    riemann: glued });
  drawBase(ctx, L.base, w, s);
}
```

```js
let last = performance.now();
function frame(now) {
  const dt = Math.min(0.05, (now - last)/1000); last = now;
  if (state.playing) {
    state.theta = (state.theta + BASE_SPEED * state.speed * dt) % (4*Math.PI);
    scrub.value = state.theta / Math.PI;
  }
  thetaVal.textContent = `θ = ${(state.theta/Math.PI).toFixed(3)}π`;
  thetaReadout.textContent = same;
  draw('sep', now); draw('glue', now);
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}
```

JavaScript 复制

```js
'use strict';
const RED = '#c8321e', BLUE = '#1f56c4', INK = '#222';
const TAU4 = 4 * Math.PI;
const cvL = document.getElementById('cvL');
const cvR = document.getElementById('cvR');
const playBtn = document.getElementById('playBtn');
const speedIn = document.getElementById('speed');
const scrub = document.getElementById('scrub');
const thetaVal = document.getElementById('thetaVal');
const thetaReadout = document.getElementById('thetaReadout');

const state = { theta: 0, playing: true, speed: 1 };
const BASE_SPEED = 0.28 * Math.PI; // θ radians per second

function pt(cx, cy, r, phi) { return [cx + r * Math.cos(phi), cy - r * Math.sin(phi)]; }

function roundRectPath(ctx, x, y, w, h, r) {
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);
  ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r);
  ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r);
  ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r);
  ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r);
  ctx.closePath();
}

function seg(ctx, x0, y0, x1, y1) {
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x0, y0); ctx.lineTo(x1, y1); ctx.stroke();
}

function arrowHead(ctx, x, y, dx, dy, size) {
  const a = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
  for (const sg of [1, -1]) {
    const ang = a + sg * 2.62;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x, y);
    ctx.lineTo(x + size * Math.cos(ang), y + size * Math.sin(ang));
    ctx.stroke();
  }
}

function arcArrow(ctx, cx, cy, r, a0, a1, color, s) {
  const n = 26;
  ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.lineWidth = 2 * s; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  ctx.beginPath();
  for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
    const a = a0 + (a1 - a0) * i / n;
    const [x, y] = pt(cx, cy, r, a);
    if (i) ctx.lineTo(x, y); else ctx.moveTo(x, y);
  }
  ctx.stroke();
  const dir = Math.sign(a1 - a0);
  const [x, y] = pt(cx, cy, r, a1);
  arrowHead(ctx, x, y, -Math.sin(a1) * dir, -Math.cos(a1) * dir, 9 * s);
}
```

Wait, barb angle 2.62 rad = 150° — barbs at ±150° from direction → 30° spread backwards ✓ size 9s.drawExp as above.drawSheet:

JavaScript 复制

```js
function drawSheet(ctx, panel, o) {
  const s = o.s;
  const cx = panel.x + panel.w / 2;
  const cy = panel.y + panel.h / 2 + 10 * s;
  const R = Math.min(panel.h * 0.355, panel.w * 0.30);

  ctx.strokeStyle = INK; ctx.lineWidth = 1.6 * s;
  roundRectPath(ctx, panel.x, panel.y, panel.w, panel.h, 10 * s); ctx.stroke();

  ctx.fillStyle = INK;
  ctx.font = `italic bold ${22 * s}px "Times New Roman", Georgia, serif`;
  ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  ctx.fillText(o.letter, panel.x + 16 * s, panel.y + 30 * s);

  if (o.note) {
    ctx.font = `${Math.max(12 * s, 9.5)}px Georgia, serif`;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#777'; ctx.textAlign = 'right';
    ctx.fillText(o.note, panel.x + panel.w - 14 * s, panel.y + 24 * s);
  }

  // axes
  ctx.strokeStyle = INK; ctx.lineWidth = 1.4 * s;
  seg(ctx, cx - R - 28 * s, cy, cx + R + 28 * s, cy);
  arrowHead(ctx, cx + R + 28 * s, cy, 1, 0, 8 * s);
  seg(ctx, cx, cy + R + 24 * s, cx, cy - R - 28 * s);
  arrowHead(ctx, cx, cy - R - 28 * s, 0, -1, 8 * s);

  // unit circle
  ctx.lineWidth = 2.2 * s;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, R, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();

  ctx.fillStyle = INK;
  ctx.font = `italic ${16 * s}px "Times New Roman", Georgia, serif`;
  ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  ctx.fillText('0', cx + 9 * s, cy + 20 * s);

  // direction arc
  arcArrow(ctx, cx, cy, R + 16 * s, o.arc[0], o.arc[1], o.color, s);

  // trail
  const SPAN = 0.55 * Math.PI, N = 34;
  for (let k = N; k >= 1; k--) {
    const th = state.theta - SPAN * k / N;
    const [x, y] = pt(cx, cy, R, o.angleFn(th));
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.3 * (1 - k / (N + 2));
    ctx.fillStyle = o.color;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, 3.2 * s, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

  // radius + dot
  const phi = o.angleFn(state.theta);
  const [dx, dy] = pt(cx, cy, R, phi);
  ctx.strokeStyle = o.color; ctx.lineWidth = 3 * s;
  seg(ctx, cx, cy, dx, dy);
  ctx.fillStyle = o.color;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(dx, dy, 8 * s, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();

  if (o.flash > 0) {
    ctx.globalAlpha = o.flash * 0.85;
    ctx.strokeStyle = o.color; ctx.lineWidth = 2 * s;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(dx, dy, 14 * s, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }

  // dot label
  const lx = dx + 18 * s * Math.cos(phi), ly = dy - 18 * s * Math.sin(phi);
  ctx.fillStyle = o.color;
  ctx.font = `italic bold ${20 * s}px "Times New Roman", Georgia, serif`;
  ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  ctx.fillText(o.dotLabel, lx, ly);

  // formula
  if (o.formulaSide === 'right') {
    drawExp(ctx, cx + R + 10 * s, cy + 4 * s, '= e', o.sup, o.color, s, 'left');
  } else {
    drawExp(ctx, cx - R - 10 * s, cy + 4 * s, '= e', o.sup, o.color, s, 'right');
  }

  if (o.riemann) {
    ctx.fillStyle = INK;
    ctx.font = `italic ${20 * s}px "Times New Roman", Georgia, serif`;
    ctx.textAlign = 'right'; ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
    ctx.fillText('Riemann Surface', panel.x + panel.w - 14 * s, panel.y + panel.h - 14 * s);
  }
}
```

JavaScript 复制

```js
function drawExp(ctx, x, y, baseStr, supStr, color, s, align) {
  const f1 = `italic ${19 * s}px "Times New Roman", Georgia, serif`;
  const f2 = `italic ${13 * s}px "Times New Roman", Georgia, serif`;
  ctx.font = f1; const w1 = ctx.measureText(baseStr).width;
  ctx.font = f2; const w2 = ctx.measureText(supStr).width;
  let x0 = x;
  if (align === 'right') x0 = x - w1 - w2;
  ctx.fillStyle = color;
  ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  ctx.font = f1; ctx.fillText(baseStr, x0, y);
  ctx.font = f2; ctx.fillText(supStr, x0 + w1 + 1, y - 9 * s);
}
```

```js
function drawBase(ctx, region, w, s) {
  const cx = w / 2;
  const r = 62 * s;
  const cy = region.y + 52 * s + r;

  ctx.fillStyle = INK;
  ctx.font = `bold ${18 * s}px Georgia, serif`;
  ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  ctx.fillText('Base space', cx, region.y + 16 * s);

  ctx.strokeStyle = INK; ctx.lineWidth = 1.4 * s;
  seg(ctx, cx, cy - r - 22 * s, cx, cy + r + 24 * s);
  seg(ctx, cx - r - 24 * s, cy, cx + r + 26 * s, cy);
  arrowHead(ctx, cx + r + 26 * s, cy, 1, 0, 8 * s);

  ctx.lineWidth = 2.2 * s;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();

  ctx.fillStyle = INK;
  ctx.font = `italic ${15 * s}px "Times New Roman", Georgia, serif`;
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  ctx.fillText('0', cx + 8 * s, cy + 18 * s);

  arcArrow(ctx, cx, cy, r + 12 * s, Math.PI * 0.42, Math.PI * 0.62, INK, s);

  // trail
  const SPAN = 0.55 * Math.PI, N = 34;
  for (let k = N; k >= 1; k--) {
    const th = state.theta - SPAN * k / N;
    const [x, y] = pt(cx, cy, r, th);
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.28 * (1 - k / (N + 2));
    ctx.fillStyle = INK;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, 2.8 * s, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

  const [dx, dy] = pt(cx, cy, r, state.theta);
  ctx.fillStyle = INK;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(dx, dy, 7 * s, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  ctx.font = `italic bold ${19 * s}px "Times New Roman", Georgia, serif`;
  ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  ctx.fillText('x', dx + 16 * s * Math.cos(state.theta), dy - 16 * s * Math.sin(state.theta));
}
```

```js
function seamGlow() {
  const tau = state.theta % TAU4;
  const d = Math.abs(tau - 2 * Math.PI);
  return Math.max(0, 1 - d / (0.35 * Math.PI));
}
```

JavaScript 复制

```js
function layoutAll(w, s) {
  const padX = 14 * s;
  const A = { x: padX, y: 12 * s, w: w - 2 * padX, h: 296 * s };
  const seam = { y: A.y + A.h, h: 54 * s };
  const B = { x: padX, y: seam.y + seam.h, w: w - 2 * padX, h: 296 * s };
  const base = { y: B.y + B.h, h: 210 * s };
  return { A, seam, B, base };
}

function prepare(cv) {
  const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  const w = cv.clientWidth || 520;
  const s = w / 520;
  const h = 880 * s;
  if (cv._w !== w || cv._dpr !== dpr) {
    cv.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(w * dpr));
    cv.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(h * dpr));
    cv.style.height = h + 'px';
    cv._w = w; cv._dpr = dpr;
  }
  const ctx = cv.getContext('2d');
  ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
  return { ctx, w, s };
}

function drawColumn(cv, glued, now) {
  const { ctx, w, s } = prepare(cv);
  const L = layoutAll(w, s);
  const glow = seamGlow();
  drawSheet(ctx, L.A, {
    s, letter: 'A', color: RED, dotLabel: 't₁',
    angleFn: th => th / 2,
    arc: [Math.PI / 6, Math.PI * 0.42],
    sup: 'iθ/2', formulaSide: 'right',
    flash: glued ? glow : 0
  });
  if (glued) drawSeam(ctx, L.seam, w, s, now, glow);
  else drawTear(ctx, L.seam, w, s);
  drawSheet(ctx, L.B, {
    s, letter: 'B', color: BLUE, dotLabel: 't₂',
    angleFn: glued ? (th => -th / 2) : (th => Math.PI + th / 2),
    arc: glued ? [-Math.PI / 6, -Math.PI * 0.44] : [Math.PI * 1.12, Math.PI * 1.38],
    sup: 'i(2π+θ)/2', formulaSide: glued ? 'left' : 'right',
    note: glued ? 'horizontal axis reversed' : null,
    riemann: glued,
    flash: glued ? glow : 0
  });
  drawBase(ctx, L.base, w, s);
}
```

- B 叶：t₂ = e^{i(2π+θ)/2}，蓝点同样逆时针，始终与 t₁ 对径

**右栏 Cross-Glued（黎曼面）**

- A 叶与左栏完全相同
- B 叶横轴镜像反转（面板内标注 "horizontal axis reversed"），t₂ 显示为顺时针，与底空间 x 的逆时针相反；公式标签也相应移到左侧

- 信息框：X = {(x,t) : x = t²}，π 为 2 重分支覆盖，t 单值全纯

**动画与交互**

- θ 以 0 → 4π 为完整周期循环（底空间两圈、分支恰好还原），状态栏实时显示 θ 值
- 两球带渐隐轨迹尾迹，顺/逆时针不靠箭头也能直接看出；另有弧形方向箭头
- 控制条：播放/暂停、速度调节（0.2×–3×）、θ 拖动条（拖动即暂停，方便定格对比两栏）

## 用户

我们讨论一下，原始的设计是否有点问题。黎曼曲面上，当t1球过了分割线，从第一叶的下边进入第二叶上边，它就应该从上面那个单位圆消失，出现在第二叶的左半轴的π那个位置，然后保持红色，继续沿下半圈绕原点旋转，到了右半轴2π也就是辐角0度处再度消失，回到它原来上面那个单位圆的起始点，继续绕行。同样，下面那个单位圆的t2球在绕到π位置时，应该消失并出现在上面那个单位圆的π位置，然后保持蓝色，继续顺时针旋转到辐角π＝0点后忽然回到自己的下面那个单位圆的出发点。

你这个直觉是对的，而且点中了原始设计的要害。但你的方案里有一个细节会和之前定下的设定打架，需要讨论清楚。分几点说：

## 一、原始设计确实有问题

右栏（交叉粘合）现在两球各自钉死在自己的叶上转整圈，和左栏的唯一差别只是方向——这恰恰**没有**表现出黎曼曲面的核心事实：

> 跟着曲面上的**同一个点**走，x 绕原点一圈（θ: 0→2π），这个点就从 A 叶被带到 B 叶；绕两圈（θ→4π）才回到出发叶。

所以"过缝换叶、保持颜色（颜色追踪的是点，不是叶）"这个改进方向完全正确。

## 二、但你描述的跳跃位置与"B 叶 x 轴反向"不自洽

B 叶是镜像显示的（你定的，箭头朝左）：**显示角 = π − 实际角**。所以 B 叶上"π 那个点"其实画在**右端**，"0 那个点"画在**左端**。t₁ 在 θ=2π 时实际值是 −1（A 叶左端，从上方到达）。按解析延拓过缝进入 B 叶后，实际值继续从 −1 往下半平面走（e^{iθ/2}，θ>2π），镜像显示出来是在 **B 叶右端**（略低于轴），而不是左端。也就是说：

- **跳跃是"交叉"的：一叶的左端 ↔ 另一叶的右端。** 这恰恰才是"交叉粘合"四个字的本意——如果是同端直跳（左→左），那是"直接粘合"，粘出来的不是这张曲面。
- 同理，蓝球过到 A 叶后只能**逆时针**走上半圈（从右端到左端），不可能"继续顺时针"：两支的实际值是 e^{iθ/2} 与 e^{i(θ/2+π)}，永远逆时针且关于原点对径（满足 x=t²）；顺时针纯粹是 B 叶镜像造成的显示效果。若让蓝球在 A 叶顺时针，它的值就变成 e^{−iθ/2}，平方是 e^{−iθ}≠x——两个球就不再是 x 的两个原像点了。

## 三、修正版的完整剧本

| θ 区间 | 红球（追踪同一点） | 蓝球（追踪另一点）
| --- | --- | --- 

| 0 → 2π | A 叶上半圈，0→π，逆时针 | B 叶下半圈，0→π，顺时针
| --- | --- | --- 
| θ = 2π | 到达 A 左端 → **闪现到 B 右端** | 到达 B 左端 → **闪现到 A 右端**
| 2π → 4π | B 叶下半圈，顺时针（右→左） | A 叶上半圈，逆时针（右→左）
| θ = 4π | 到达 B 左端 → **跳回 A 右端**，循环 | 到达 A 左端 → **跳回 B 右端**，循环

这个版本的性质：任何时刻每叶恰有一球；A 叶永远逆时针、B 叶永远顺时针（现有的方向箭头、镜像轴、公式全部不用动）；θ=2π 时两球同时滑到各自左端、同时交叉闪跳到对方右端——这是全片最戏剧化的一刻，正好演示换叶。左栏保持不变作对照。

## 四、需要你拍板

1. 采用**修正版**（交叉跳，一切自洽，推荐）还是**字面版**（同端直跳、蓝球在 A 叶顺时针——视觉更顺滑，但蓝球不满足 x=t²，且 B 叶反向轴得撤掉）？
2. 若用修正版，要不要把两叶之间的虚线缝改成 **X 形交叉双线**（左端连右端），把"交叉"直接画出来？

你定，我再动手。

```js
function drawBall(ctx, s, getPos, color, label, flash) {
  const SPAN = 0.55 * Math.PI, N = 34;
  for (let k = N; k >= 1; k--) {
    const p = getPos(state.theta - SPAN * k / N);
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.3 * (1 - k / (N+2));
    ctx.fillStyle = color;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, 3.2*s, 0, 2π); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  const p = getPos(state.theta);
  ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.lineWidth = 3*s;
  seg(ctx, p.cx, p.cy, p.x, p.y);
  ctx.fillStyle = color;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, 8*s, 0, 2π); ctx.fill();
  if (flash > 0) { ring }
  // label
  const phi = Math.atan2(p.cy - p.y, p.x - p.cx); // displayed angle (canvas y down)
  ctx.font = italic bold 20s; textAlign center; baseline middle; fillStyle color;
  ctx.fillText(label, p.x + 15*s*Math.cos(phi), p.y - 15*s*Math.sin(phi) - 10*s);
}
```

phi from atan2(cy−y, x−cx): since pt() has y = cy − R sin φ, cy − y = R sin φ, x − cx = R cos φ → atan2(R sin φ, R cos φ) = φ ✓.getPos builders:

JavaScript 复制

```js
function makeGetter(centers, angleFn, useSeamRule) {
  return function(th) {
    const phi = angleFn(th);
    let c;
    if (useSeamRule) {
      const n = ((phi % (2π)) + 2π) % (2π);
      c = n < Math.PI ? centers.A : centers.B;
    } else {
      c = centers.fixed;
    }
    const p = pt(c.cx, c.cy, c.R, phi);
    return { x: p[0], y: p[1], cx: c.cx, cy: c.cy };
  };
}
```

So two big edit blocks: (1) replace drawSheet function with new functions; (2) replace drawSeam; (3) replace drawColumn. Let me get the current exact text of those blocks from what I wrote earlier (with edits applied). Current drawSheet (after xFlip edit):

JavaScript 复制

```js
/* ---------- one sheet panel (A or B) ---------- */
function drawSheet(ctx, panel, o) {
  const s = o.s;
  const cx = panel.x + panel.w / 2;
  const cy = panel.y + panel.h / 2 + 10 * s;
  const R = Math.min(panel.h * 0.355, panel.w * 0.30);

  ctx.strokeStyle = INK; ctx.lineWidth = 1.6 * s;
  roundRectPath(ctx, panel.x, panel.y, panel.w, panel.h, 10 * s); ctx.stroke();

  ctx.fillStyle = INK;
  ctx.font = 'italic bold ' + (22 * s) + 'px "Times New Roman", Georgia, serif';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  ctx.fillText(o.letter, panel.x + 16 * s, panel.y + 30 * s);

  if (o.note) {
    ctx.font = Math.max(12 * s, 9.5) + 'px Georgia, serif';
    ctx.fillStyle = '#777'; ctx.textAlign = 'right';
    ctx.fillText(o.note, panel.x + panel.w - 14 * s, panel.y + 24 * s);
  }

  // axes
  ctx.strokeStyle = INK; ctx.lineWidth = 1.4 * s;
  seg(ctx, cx - R - 28 * s, cy, cx + R + 28 * s, cy);
  if (o.xFlip) arrowHead(ctx, cx - R - 28 * s, cy, -1, 0, 8 * s);
  else arrowHead(ctx, cx + R + 28 * s, cy, 1, 0, 8 * s);
  seg(ctx, cx, cy + R + 24 * s, cx, cy - R - 28 * s);
  arrowHead(ctx, cx, cy - R - 28 * s, 0, -1, 8 * s);

  // unit circle
  ctx.lineWidth = 2.2 * s;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, R, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();

  ctx.fillStyle = INK;
  ctx.font = 'italic ' + (16 * s) + 'px "Times New Roman", Georgia, serif';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  ctx.fillText('0', cx + 9 * s, cy + 20 * s);

  // direction arrow (CCW or CW)
  arcArrow(ctx, cx, cy, R + 16 * s, o.arc[0], o.arc[1], o.color, s);

  // fading trail
  const SPAN = 0.55 * Math.PI, N = 34;
  for (let k = N; k >= 1; k--) {
    const th = state.theta - SPAN * k / N;
    const p = pt(cx, cy, R, o.angleFn(th));
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.3 * (1 - k / (N + 2));
    ctx.fillStyle = o.color;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p[0], p[1], 3.2 * s, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

  // radius line + dot
  const phi = o.angleFn(state.theta);
  const d = pt(cx, cy, R, phi);
  ctx.strokeStyle = o.color; ctx.lineWidth = 3 * s;
  seg(ctx, cx, cy, d[0], d[1]);
  ctx.fillStyle = o.color;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(d[0], d[1], 8 * s, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();

  if (o.flash > 0) {
    ctx.globalAlpha = o.flash * 0.85;
    ctx.strokeStyle = o.color; ctx.lineWidth = 2 * s;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(d[0], d[1], 14 * s, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }

  // dot label
  ctx.fillStyle = o.color;
  ctx.font = 'italic bold ' + (20 * s) + 'px "Times New Roman", Georgia, serif';
  ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  ctx.fillText(o.dotLabel, d[0] + 15 * s * Math.cos(phi), d[1] - 15 * s * Math.sin(phi) - 10 * s);

  // branch formula
  if (o.formulaSide === 'right') {
    drawExp(ctx, cx + R + 34 * s, cy - 2 * s, '= e', o.sup, o.color, s, 'left');
  } else {
    drawExp(ctx, cx - R - 34 * s, cy - 2 * s, '= e', o.sup, o.color, s, 'right');
  }

  if (o.riemann) {
    ctx.fillStyle = INK;
    ctx.font = 'italic ' + (20 * s) + 'px "Times New Roman", Georgia, serif';
    ctx.textAlign = 'right'; ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
    ctx.fillText('Riemann Surface', panel.x + panel.w - 14 * s, panel.y + panel.h - 14 * s);
  }
}
```

Replace with drawSheetFrame + makeGetter + drawBall. New code:

JavaScript 复制

```js
/* ---------- one sheet panel (A or B): static geometry ---------- */
function drawSheetFrame(ctx, panel, o) {
  const s = o.s;
  const cx = panel.x + panel.w / 2;
  const cy = panel.y + panel.h / 2 + 10 * s;
  const R = Math.min(panel.h * 0.355, panel.w * 0.30);

  if (o.border) {
    ctx.strokeStyle = INK; ctx.lineWidth = 1.6 * s;
    roundRectPath(ctx, panel.x, panel.y, panel.w, panel.h, 10 * s); ctx.stroke();
  }

  ctx.fillStyle = INK;
  ctx.font = 'italic bold ' + (22 * s) + 'px "Times New Roman", Georgia, serif';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  ctx.fillText(o.letter, o.letterX, o.letterY);

  // axes (arrow to the right)
  ctx.strokeStyle = INK; ctx.lineWidth = 1.4 * s;
  seg(ctx, cx - R - 28 * s, cy, cx + R + 28 * s, cy);
  arrowHead(ctx, cx + R + 28 * s, cy, 1, 0, 8 * s);
  seg(ctx, cx, cy + R + 24 * s, cx, cy - R - 28 * s);
  arrowHead(ctx, cx, cy - R - 28 * s, 0, -1, 8 * s);

  // unit circle
  ctx.lineWidth = 2.2 * s;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, R, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();

  ctx.fillStyle = INK;
  ctx.font = 'italic ' + (16 * s) + 'px "Times New Roman", Georgia, serif';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  ctx.fillText('0', cx + 9 * s, cy + 20 * s);

  // direction arrow (counterclockwise)
  arcArrow(ctx, cx, cy, R + 16 * s, o.arc[0], o.arc[1], o.color, s);

  // branch formula
  if (o.formulaSide === 'right') {
    drawExp(ctx, cx + R + 34 * s, cy - 2 * s, '= e', o.sup, o.color, s, 'left');
  } else {
    drawExp(ctx, cx - R - 34 * s, cy - 2 * s, '= e', o.sup, o.color, s, 'right');
  }

  if (o.riemann) {
    ctx.fillStyle = INK;
    ctx.font = 'italic ' + (20 * s) + 'px "Times New Roman", Georgia, serif';
    ctx.textAlign = 'right'; ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
    ctx.fillText('Riemann Surface', o.riemann.x, o.riemann.y);
  }

  return { cx: cx, cy: cy, R: R };
}

/* ---------- moving ball (trail + radius + dot + label) ---------- */
function drawBall(ctx, s, getPos, color, label, flash) {
  const SPAN = 0.55 * Math.PI, N = 34;
  for (let k = N; k >= 1; k--) {
    const p = getPos(state.theta - SPAN * k / N);
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.3 * (1 - k / (N + 2));
    ctx.fillStyle = color;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, 3.2 * s, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

  const p = getPos(state.theta);
  ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.lineWidth = 3 * s;
  seg(ctx, p.cx, p.cy, p.x, p.y);
  ctx.fillStyle = color;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, 8 * s, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();

  if (flash > 0) {
    ctx.globalAlpha = flash * 0.85;
    ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.lineWidth = 2 * s;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, 14 * s, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }

  const phi = Math.atan2(p.cy - p.y, p.x - p.cx);
  ctx.fillStyle = color;
  ctx.font = 'italic bold ' + (20 * s) + 'px "Times New Roman", Georgia, serif';
  ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  ctx.fillText(label, p.x + 15 * s * Math.cos(phi), p.y - 15 * s * Math.sin(phi) - 10 * s);
}

/* position getters: fixed sheet (separate view) or seam-crossing (glued view) */
function fixedGetter(c, angleFn) {
  return function (th) {
    const d = pt(c.cx, c.cy, c.R, angleFn(th));
    return { x: d[0], y: d[1], cx: c.cx, cy: c.cy };
  };
}

function seamGetter(centers, angleFn) {
  return function (th) {
    const phi = angleFn(th);
    const n = ((phi % (Math.PI * 2)) + Math.PI * 2) % (Math.PI * 2);
    const c = n < Math.PI ? centers.A : centers.B;
    const d = pt(c.cx, c.cy, c.R, phi);
    return { x: d[0], y: d[1], cx: c.cx, cy: c.cy };
  };
}
```

```js
function drawSeam(ctx, seam, x0, x1, s) {
  const y = seam.y + seam.h / 2;
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#333'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.6 * s;
  ctx.setLineDash([7 * s, 6 * s]);
  seg(ctx, x0, y, x1, y);
  ctx.setLineDash([]);

  ctx.lineWidth = 2 * s;
  ctx.strokeStyle = BLUE;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x0, y, 6 * s, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.strokeStyle = RED;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x1, y, 6 * s, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();

  ctx.font = Math.max(12.5 * s, 10) + 'px Georgia, serif';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  ctx.fillStyle = BLUE;
  ctx.fillText('lower edge of A', x0 + 12 * s, y + 14 * s);
  ctx.fillText('glued to upper edge of B', x0 + 12 * s, y + 28 * s);
  ctx.textAlign = 'right';
  ctx.fillStyle = RED;
  ctx.fillText('upper edge of A', x1 - 12 * s, y - 26 * s);
  ctx.fillText('glued to lower edge of B', x1 - 12 * s, y - 12 * s);
}
```

```js
function drawColumn(cv, glued, now) {
  const pr = prepare(cv);
  const ctx = pr.ctx, w = pr.w, s = pr.s;
  const L = layoutAll(w, s);
  const glow = seamGlow();

  if (!glued) {
    const cA = drawSheetFrame(ctx, L.A, {
      s: s, letter: 'A', border: true,
      letterX: L.A.x + 16 * s, letterY: L.A.y + 30 * s,
      arc: [Math.PI / 6, Math.PI * 0.42], color: RED,
      sup: 'iθ/2', formulaSide: 'right'
    });
    const cB = drawSheetFrame(ctx, L.B, {
      s: s, letter: 'B', border: true,
      letterX: L.B.x + 16 * s, letterY: L.B.y + 30 * s,
      arc: [Math.PI * 1.12, Math.PI * 1.38], color: BLUE,
      sup: 'i(2π+θ)/2', formulaSide: 'left'
    });
    drawBall(ctx, s, fixedGetter(cA, function (th) { return th / 2; }), RED, 't₁', 0);
    drawBall(ctx, s, fixedGetter(cB, function (th) { return th / 2 + Math.PI; }), BLUE, 't₂', 0);
  } else {
    // one connected surface: single panel, dashed seam in the middle
    const box = { x: L.A.x, y: L.A.y, w: L.A.w, h: (L.B.y + L.B.h) - L.A.y };
    ctx.strokeStyle = INK; ctx.lineWidth = 1.6 * s;
    roundRectPath(ctx, box.x, box.y, box.w, box.h, 10 * s); ctx.stroke();
    drawSeam(ctx, L.seam, box.x + 10 * s, box.x + box.w - 10 * s, s);

    const cA = drawSheetFrame(ctx, L.A, {
      s: s, letter: 'A', border: false,
      letterX: box.x + 16 * s, letterY: box.y + 30 * s,
      arc: [Math.PI / 6, Math.PI * 0.42], color: RED,
      sup: 'iθ/2', formulaSide: 'right'
    });
    const cB = drawSheetFrame(ctx, L.B, {
      s: s, letter: 'B', border: false,
      letterX: box.x + 16 * s, letterY: L.B.y + 30 * s,
      arc: [Math.PI * 1.12, Math.PI * 1.38], color: BLUE,
      sup: 'i(2π+θ)/2', formulaSide: 'left',
      riemann: { x: box.x + box.w - 14 * s, y: box.y + box.h - 14 * s }
    });
    const centers = { A: cA, B: cB };
    drawBall(ctx, s, seamGetter(centers, function (th) { return th / 2; }), RED, 't₁', glow);
    drawBall(ctx, s, seamGetter(centers, function (th) { return th / 2 + Math.PI; }), BLUE, 't₂', glow);
  }

  drawBase(ctx, L.base, w, s);
}
```

JavaScript 复制

```js
function drawSeam(ctx, seam, w, s) {
  const y = seam.y + seam.h / 2;
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#333'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.6 * s;
  ctx.setLineDash([7 * s, 6 * s]);
  seg(ctx, 10 * s, y, w - 10 * s, y);
  ctx.setLineDash([]);

  ctx.lineWidth = 2 * s;
  ctx.strokeStyle = BLUE;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(10 * s, y, 6 * s, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.strokeStyle = RED;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(w - 10 * s, y, 6 * s, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();

  ctx.font = Math.max(12.5 * s, 10) + 'px Georgia, serif';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  ctx.fillStyle = BLUE;
  ctx.fillText('lower edge of A', 20 * s, y + 14 * s);
  ctx.fillText('glued to upper edge of B', 20 * s, y + 28 * s);
  ctx.textAlign = 'right';
  ctx.fillStyle = RED;
  ctx.fillText('upper edge of A', w - 20 * s, y - 26 * s);
  ctx.fillText('glued to lower edge of B', w - 20 * s, y - 12 * s);
}
```

JavaScript 复制

```js
function drawColumn(cv, glued, now) {
  const pr = prepare(cv);
  const ctx = pr.ctx, w = pr.w, s = pr.s;
  const L = layoutAll(w, s);
  const glow = seamGlow();

  drawSheet(ctx, L.A, {
    s: s, letter: 'A', color: RED, dotLabel: 't₁',
    angleFn: function (th) { return th / 2; },
    arc: [Math.PI / 6, Math.PI * 0.42],
    sup: 'iθ/2', formulaSide: 'right',
    note: null, riemann: false, flash: glued ? glow : 0
  });

  if (glued) drawSeam(ctx, L.seam, w, s);

  drawSheet(ctx, L.B, {
    s: s, letter: 'B', color: BLUE, dotLabel: 't₂',
    angleFn: glued ? function (th) { return -th / 2; }
                   : function (th) { return Math.PI + th / 2; },
    arc: glued ? [-Math.PI / 6, -Math.PI * 0.44]
               : [Math.PI * 1.12, Math.PI * 1.38],
    sup: 'i(2π+θ)/2', formulaSide: glued ? 'right' : 'left',
    note: glued ? 'horizontal axis reversed' : null,
    xFlip: glued,
    riemann: glued, flash: glued ? glow : 0
  });

  drawBase(ctx, L.base, w, s);
}
```

Version saved: abcb567. Summarize the changes for the user.

改完了，新版本号：abcb567。这次按你的判断重构了右栏：**轴与粘合**

- B 叶 x 轴箭头改回朝右，撤掉了“horizontal axis reversed”标注——交叉粘合由“岸”的交换体现（A 上半 ↔ B 下半），不再靠镜像，镜像确实会把交叉变成“下粘下”的假粘合。
- t₂ 的 `= e^{i(2π+θ)/2}` 标签跟着移到 B 叶左半轴（无箭头一侧）。

**两框打通**

- 右栏 A、B 合并为一个大圆角框，中间只隔一根虚线缝（两端蓝/红小圈和粘合规则文字保留），直观表示“一张曲面”而非两张平面。

**过缝换叶、保持颜色（核心改动）**

- 红球 t₁：A 叶上半圈 0→π 逆时针 → 到左端后直行进入 B 叶左端，沿下半圈继续逆时针到 0 → 回 A 叶右端，循环。
- 蓝球 t₂：B 叶下半圈 π→0 逆时针 → 到右端后直行进入 A 叶右端，沿上半圈逆时针到 π → 回 B 叶左端，循环。
- 过缝瞬间位置与切向都连续（到左端时两球切向都朝下、到右端时都朝上），没有跳接——正是解析延拓的连续性；且任意时刻两球关于原点对径、满足 x = t²，每叶恰有一球。θ = 2π 时两球同时过缝（红 A左→B左、蓝 B右→A右），有闪圈提示。
- 左栏保持不变作对照：两球各在自己的叶上转整圈、永不换叶。

一个连带变化再提醒一下：撤掉镜像后蓝球在 θ=0 位于 B 叶左端（π 处）出发，这是 t₂ = −t₁ 的必然结果；两栏在 θ∈[0,2π] 时画面完全一致，差异从第一次过缝才开始显现——这正好就是最要的对比效果。

版本保存成功 commit 9cf45dc

完成，新版本号：9cf45dc。右栏（黎曼曲面）两个单位圆的方向箭头现在跟随当前球变色：

- θ∈[0, 2π]：A 叶红球 → A 箭头红色；B 叶蓝球 → B 箭头蓝色；
- θ 过 2π 换叶后：A 叶蓝球 → A 箭头变蓝；B 叶红球 → B 箭头变红；
- θ 过 4π 回到起始状态，颜色随之切回。

左栏两叶球不换叶，箭头颜色保持红/蓝不变。公式标签（A 红 B 蓝）仍固定在各自的叶上，未跟着换——如果你也希望公式跟球走，说一声就改。

```js
const upBias = Math.sin(phi) >= 0 ? 8 * s : 0;
ctx.fillText(label, p.x + 22 * s * Math.cos(phi), p.y - 22 * s * Math.sin(phi) - upBias);
```

Done. Report concisely in Chinese: the two fixes + the arc relocation, version ID 3cffc78.

两个小瑕疵都修好了，版本已保存（版本号 3cffc78）。具体改动：

1. **标签被遮挡**：`t₁`/`t₂` 标签的偏移量加大（径向外移 22s），并改成智能抬升——球在上半圈时标签额外上抬避开坐标轴，球在下半圈时纯径向外移。这样无论球转到哪个角度，标签与球体之间都有明显空隙，不会再被球压住。
2. **多余的圈**：换叶时刻球外的提示圆环（连同它的闪光逻辑）整体删除，过缝时球体不再出现任何圆圈。

另外截图复查时发现一个同类隐患：球经过方向箭头所在扇区时，标签会蹭到箭头尖端。由于粘合视图里球在 A 上只走上半圈、在 B 上只走下半圈，我把右栏两个方向箭头挪到了球永远不会到达的对面半圈（A 的箭头在右下，B 的箭头在左上），颜色仍跟随当前所在球的颜色，逆时针指向保持不变。左栏未动。
